Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cell_biology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 438. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 924 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cell_biology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^438 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 924-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
